Original document: luxurystore/synaptic-0.91.7/INSTALL

Installation Instructions
*************************

   Copyright (C) 1994-1996, 1999-2002, 2004-2016 Free Software
Foundation, Inc.

   Copying and distribution of this file, with or without modification,
are permitted in any medium without royalty provided the copyright
notice and this notice are preserved.  This file is offered as-is,
without warranty of any kind.

Basic Installation
==================

   Briefly, the shell command './configure && make && make install'
should configure, build, and install this package.  The following
more-detailed instructions are generic; see the 'README' file for
instructions specific to this package.  Some packages provide this
'INSTALL' file but do not implement all of the features documented
below.  The lack of an optional feature in a given package is not
necessarily a bug.  More recommendations for GNU packages can be found
in *note Makefile Conventions: (standards)Makefile Conventions.

   The 'configure' shell script attempts to guess correct values for
various system-dependent variables used during compilation.  It uses
those values to create a 'Makefile' in each directory of the package.
It may also create one or more '.h' files containing system-dependent
definitions.  Finally, it creates a shell script 'config.status' that
you can run in the future to recreate the current configuration, and a
file 'config.log' containing compiler output (useful mainly for
debugging 'configure').

   It can also use an optional file (typically called 'config.cache' and
enabled with '--cache-file=config.cache' or simply '-C') that saves the
results of its tests to speed up reconfiguring.  Caching is disabled by
default to prevent problems with accidental use of stale cache files.

   If you need to do unusual things to compile the package, please try
to figure out how 'configure' could check whether to do them, and mail
diffs or instructions to the address given in the 'README' so they can
be considered for the next release.  If you are using the cache, and at
some point 'config.cache' contains results you don't want to keep, you
may remove or edit it.

   The file 'configure.ac' (or 'configure.in') is used to create
'configure' by a program called 'autoconf'.  You need 'configure.ac' if
you want to change it or regenerate 'configure' using a newer version of
'autoconf'.

   The simplest way to compile this package is:

  1. 'cd' to the directory containing the package's source code and type
     './configure' to configure the package for your system.

     Running 'configure' might take a while.  While running, it prints
     some messages telling which features it is checking for.

  2. Type 'make' to compile the package.

  3. Optionally, type 'make check' to run any self-tests that come with
     the package, generally using the just-built uninstalled binaries.

  4. Type 'make install' to install the programs and any data files and
     documentation.  When installing into a prefix owned by root, it is
     recommended that the package be configured and built as a regular
     user, and only the 'make install' phase executed with root
     privileges.

  5. Optionally, type 'make installcheck' to repeat any self-tests, but
     this time using the binaries in their final installed location.
     This target does not install anything.  Running this target as a
     regular user, particularly if the prior 'make install' required
     root privileges, verifies that the installation completed
     correctly.

  6. You can remove the program binaries and object files from the
     source code directory by typing 'make clean'.  To also remove the
     files that 'configure' created (so you can compile the package for
     a different kind of computer), type 'make distclean'.  There is
     also a 'make maintainer-clean' target, but that is intended mainly
     for the package's developers.  If you use it, you may have to get
     all sorts of other programs in order to regenerate files that came
     with the distribution.

  7. Often, you can also type 'make uninstall' to remove the installed
     files again.  In practice, not all packages have tested that
     uninstallation works correctly, even though it is required by the
     GNU Coding Standards.

  8. Some packages, particularly those that use Automake, provide 'make
     distcheck', which can by used by developers to test that all other
     targets like 'make install' and 'make uninstall' work correctly.
     This target is generally not run by end users.

Compilers and Options
=====================

   Some systems require unusual options for compilation or linking that
the 'configure' script does not know about.  Run './configure --help'
for details on some of the pertinent environment variables.

   You can give 'configure' initial values for configuration parameters
by setting variables in the command line or in the environment.  Here is
an example:

     ./configure CC=c99 CFLAGS=-g LIBS=-lposix

   *Note Defining Variables::, for more details.

Compiling For Multiple Architectures
====================================

   You can compile the package for more than one kind of computer at the
same time, by placing the object files for each architecture in their
own directory.  To do this, you can use GNU 'make'.  'cd' to the
directory where you want the object files and executables to go and run
the 'configure' script.  'configure' automatically checks for the source
code in the directory that 'configure' is in and in '..'.  This is known
as a "VPATH" build.

   With a non-GNU 'make', it is safer to compile the package for one
architecture at a time in the source code directory.  After you have
installed the package for one architecture, use 'make distclean' before
reconfiguring for another architecture.

   On MacOS X 10.5 and later systems, you can create libraries and
executables that work on multiple system types--known as "fat" or
"universal" binaries--by specifying multiple '-arch' options to the
compiler but only a single '-arch' option to the preprocessor.  Like
this:

     ./configure CC="gcc -arch i386 -arch x86_64 -arch ppc -arch ppc64" \
                 CXX="g++ -arch i386 -arch x86_64 -arch ppc -arch ppc64" \
                 CPP="gcc -E" CXXCPP="g++ -E"

   This is not guaranteed to produce working output in all cases, you
may have to build one architecture at a time and combine the results
using the 'lipo' tool if you have problems.

Installation Names
==================

   By default, 'make install' installs the package's commands under
'/usr/local/bin', include files under '/usr/local/include', etc.  You
can specify an installation prefix other than '/usr/local' by giving
'configure' the option '--prefix=PREFIX', where PREFIX must be an
absolute file name.

   You can specify separate installation prefixes for
architecture-specific files and architecture-independent files.  If you
pass the option '--exec-prefix=PREFIX' to 'configure', the package uses
PREFIX as the prefix for installing programs and libraries.
Documentation and other data files still use the regular prefix.

   In addition, if you use an unusual directory layout you can give
options like '--bindir=DIR' to specify different values for particular
kinds of files.  Run 'configure --help' for a list of the directories
you can set and what kinds of files go in them.  In general, the default
for these options is expressed in terms of '${prefix}', so that
specifying just '--prefix' will affect all of the other directory
specifications that were not explicitly provided.

   The most portable way to affect installation locations is to pass the
correct locations to 'configure'; however, many packages provide one or
both of the following shortcuts of passing variable assignments to the
'make install' command line to change installation locations without
having to reconfigure or recompile.

   The first method involves providing an override variable for each
affected directory.  For example, 'make install
prefix=/alternate/directory' will choose an alternate location for all
directory configuration variables that were expressed in terms of
'${prefix}'.  Any directories that were specified during 'configure',
but not in terms of '${prefix}', must each be overridden at install time
for the entire installation to be relocated.  The approach of makefile
variable overrides for each directory variable is required by the GNU
Coding Standards, and ideally causes no recompilation.  However, some
platforms have known limitations with the semantics of shared libraries
that end up requiring recompilation when using this method, particularly
noticeable in packages that use GNU Libtool.

   The second method involves providing the 'DESTDIR' variable.  For
example, 'make install DESTDIR=/alternate/directory' will prepend
'/alternate/directory' before all installation names.  The approach of
'DESTDIR' overrides is not required by the GNU Coding Standards, and
does not work on platforms that have drive letters.  On the other hand,
it does better at avoiding recompilation issues, and works well even
when some directory options were not specified in terms of '${prefix}'
at 'configure' time.

Optional Features
=================

   If the package supports it, you can cause programs to be installed
with an extra prefix or suffix on their names by giving 'configure' the
option '--program-prefix=PREFIX' or '--program-suffix=SUFFIX'.

   Some packages pay attention to '--enable-FEATURE' options to
'configure', where FEATURE indicates an optional part of the package.
They may also pay attention to '--with-PACKAGE' options, where PACKAGE
is something like 'gnu-as' or 'x' (for the X Window System).  The
'README' should mention any '--enable-' and '--with-' options that the
package recognizes.

   For packages that use the X Window System, 'configure' can usually
find the X include and library files automatically, but if it doesn't,
you can use the 'configure' options '--x-includes=DIR' and
'--x-libraries=DIR' to specify their locations.

   Some packages offer the ability to configure how verbose the
execution of 'make' will be.  For these packages, running './configure
--enable-silent-rules' sets the default to minimal output, which can be
overridden with 'make V=1'; while running './configure
--disable-silent-rules' sets the default to verbose, which can be
overridden with 'make V=0'.

Particular systems
==================

   On HP-UX, the default C compiler is not ANSI C compatible.  If GNU CC
is not installed, it is recommended to use the following options in
order to use an ANSI C compiler:

     ./configure CC="cc -Ae -D_XOPEN_SOURCE=500"

and if that doesn't work, install pre-built binaries of GCC for HP-UX.

   HP-UX 'make' updates targets which have the same time stamps as their
prerequisites, which makes it generally unusable when shipped generated
files such as 'configure' are involved.  Use GNU 'make' instead.

   On OSF/1 a.k.a. Tru64, some versions of the default C compiler cannot
parse its '<wchar.h>' header file.  The option '-nodtk' can be used as a
workaround.  If GNU CC is not installed, it is therefore recommended to
try

     ./configure CC="cc"

and if that doesn't work, try

     ./configure CC="cc -nodtk"

   On Solaris, don't put '/usr/ucb' early in your 'PATH'.  This
directory contains several dysfunctional programs; working variants of
these programs are available in '/usr/bin'.  So, if you need '/usr/ucb'
in your 'PATH', put it _after_ '/usr/bin'.

   On Haiku, software installed for all users goes in '/boot/common',
not '/usr/local'.  It is recommended to use the following options:

     ./configure --prefix=/boot/common

Specifying the System Type
==========================

   There may be some features 'configure' cannot figure out
automatically, but needs to determine by the type of machine the package
will run on.  Usually, assuming the package is built to be run on the
_same_ architectures, 'configure' can figure that out, but if it prints
a message saying it cannot guess the machine type, give it the
'--build=TYPE' option.  TYPE can either be a short name for the system
type, such as 'sun4', or a canonical name which has the form:

     CPU-COMPANY-SYSTEM

where SYSTEM can have one of these forms:

     OS
     KERNEL-OS

   See the file 'config.sub' for the possible values of each field.  If
'config.sub' isn't included in this package, then this package doesn't
need to know the machine type.

   If you are _building_ compiler tools for cross-compiling, you should
use the option '--target=TYPE' to select the type of system they will
produce code for.

   If you want to _use_ a cross compiler, that generates code for a
platform different from the build platform, you should specify the
"host" platform (i.e., that on which the generated programs will
eventually be run) with '--host=TYPE'.

Sharing Defaults
================

   If you want to set default values for 'configure' scripts to share,
you can create a site shell script called 'config.site' that gives
default values for variables like 'CC', 'cache_file', and 'prefix'.
'configure' looks for 'PREFIX/share/config.site' if it exists, then
'PREFIX/etc/config.site' if it exists.  Or, you can set the
'CONFIG_SITE' environment variable to the location of the site script.
A warning: not all 'configure' scripts look for a site script.

Defining Variables
==================

   Variables not defined in a site shell script can be set in the
environment passed to 'configure'.  However, some packages may run
configure again during the build, and the customized values of these
variables may be lost.  In order to avoid this problem, you should set
them in the 'configure' command line, using 'VAR=value'.  For example:

     ./configure CC=/usr/local2/bin/gcc

causes the specified 'gcc' to be used as the C compiler (unless it is
overridden in the site shell script).

Unfortunately, this technique does not work for 'CONFIG_SHELL' due to an
Autoconf limitation.  Until the limitation is lifted, you can use this
workaround:

     CONFIG_SHELL=/bin/bash ./configure CONFIG_SHELL=/bin/bash

'configure' Invocation
======================

   'configure' recognizes the following options to control how it
operates.

'--help'
'-h'
     Print a summary of all of the options to 'configure', and exit.

'--help=short'
'--help=recursive'
     Print a summary of the options unique to this package's
     'configure', and exit.  The 'short' variant lists options used only
     in the top level, while the 'recursive' variant lists options also
     present in any nested packages.

'--version'
'-V'
     Print the version of Autoconf used to generate the 'configure'
     script, and exit.

'--cache-file=FILE'
     Enable the cache: use and save the results of the tests in FILE,
     traditionally 'config.cache'.  FILE defaults to '/dev/null' to
     disable caching.

'--config-cache'
'-C'
     Alias for '--cache-file=config.cache'.

'--quiet'
'--silent'
'-q'
     Do not print messages saying which checks are being made.  To
     suppress all normal output, redirect it to '/dev/null' (any error
     messages will still be shown).

'--srcdir=DIR'
     Look for the package's source code in directory DIR.  Usually
     'configure' can determine that directory automatically.

'--prefix=DIR'
     Use DIR as the installation prefix.  *note Installation Names:: for
     more details, including other options available for fine-tuning the
     installation locations.

'--no-create'
'-n'
     Run the configure checks, but stop before creating any output
     files.

'configure' also accepts some other, not widely useful, options.  Run
'configure --help' for more details.


Original document: luxurystore/synaptic-0.91.7/man/synaptic.8

.\" First parameter, NAME, should be all caps
.\" Second parameter, SECTION, should be 1-8, maybe w/ subsection
.\" other parameters are allowed: see man(7), man(1)
.TH SYNAPTIC 8 "Jan 24, 2025"
.\" Please adjust this date whenever revising the manpage.
.\"
.\" Some roff macros, for reference:
.\" .nh        disable hyphenation
.\" .hy        enable hyphenation
.\" .ad l      left justify
.\" .ad b      justify to both left and right margins
.\" .nf        disable filling
.\" .fi        enable filling
.\" .br        insert line break
.\" .sp <n>    insert n+1 empty lines
.\" for manpage-specific macros, see man(7)
.SH NAME
synaptic \- graphical management of software packages
.SH SYNOPSIS
\fBsynaptic\fP [options]
.br
.SH DESCRIPTION
Synaptic is a frontend for the apt package managent system.
It allows you to perform all actions of the command line tool
apt-get in a graphical environment. This includes installing,
upgrading, downgrading  and removing of single packages or
even upgrading your whole system.
.PP
A manual with detailed instructions can be found in the help
menu of Synaptic.

.SH OPTIONS
Synaptic accepts all of the standard GTK toolkit command line
options as well as the following:
.TP
\fB-f\fR, \fB\-\-filter-file\fR=\fIfilename\fR
specify an alternative filter settings file
.TP
\fB-i\fR, \fB\-\-initial-filter\fR=\fIint\fR
apply the filter with number \fIint\fR at startup
.TP
\fB-o\fR, \fB\-\-option\fR=\fIoption\fR
set an internal option (experts only)

.SH AUTHORS
Synaptic was originally developed by Alfredo K. Kojima
<kojima@conectiva.com.br>. His last official release was 0.16. Michael
Vogt <mvo@debian.org> took over his CVS version, that already included a
nearly complete port to GTK. Michael completed the port and added new
features. See the \fINEWS\fR file for the user visible changes from
that point on. Conectiva is still involved in the development of
synaptic. Gustavo Niemeyer <niemeyer@conectiva.com> is doing a
great deal of work.
.PP
All development is done at https://github.com/mvo5/synaptic
.PP
This manual page was originally written by Wybo Dekker <wybo@servalys.nl> and
Michael Vogt <mvo@debian.org> and modified by Sebastian Heinlein
<sebastian.heinlein@campus.lmu.de>.

the files named COPYING.

<sect1 id="synaptic-introduction"> 
	<title>Uvod</title> 
	<para>
		&app; omogućuje vam instalaciju i upravljanje instaliranim softverom na vašem računalu. 
		Softver se isporučuje pomoću tzv. <emphasis>paketa</emphasis>. Jedna aplikacija može
		postojati u nekoliko paketa: npr. <application>Mozilla Internet Suite</application> je
		pakirana u <emphasis>mozilla-browser</emphasis>, koja sadrži preglednik, i <emphasis>mozilla-mail
		</emphasis>, koja sadrži klijent za poštu (ovaj primjer se odnosi na Debian GNU/Linux 
		 i može biti drugačija zavisno o vašoj distribuciji). 
	</para>
	<para>
		Gotovo sve aplikacije ponovno koriste funkcionalnost drugih aplikacija ili biblioteka 
		(biblioteke samo pružaju funkcije ostalim bibliotekama ili aplikacijama i nisu zasebne aplikacije)
		kako bi izbjegle dvostruke radnje. Stoga većina paketa ovisi o drugim paketima. &app; automatski razrješava <emphasis>
		zavisnosti</emphasis> umjesto vas. 
	</para>
	<para>
		Tehnički &app; je temeljen na paketnom upravitelju <application>APT</application> i pruža funkcije, koje su slične 
		onima u alatu naredbenog redka <command>apt-get</command> u grafičkom okruženju.
	</para>
	<para>
		Opširnije &app; pruža sljedeće značajke: 

	        <itemizedlist>
		        <listitem>
			        <para>
				        Instalacija, uklanjanje, podešavanja, nadogradnja i vraćanje prijašnje inačice jednoga ili više paketa.
	                        </para>
		        </listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Potpuna nadogradnja vašeg sustava.
	                        </para>
		        </listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
	                                Upravljanje paketnim repozitorijima.
		                </para>
	                </listitem>
		        <listitem>
			        <para>
				        Pretraživanje paketa prema nazivu, opisu i nekoliko ostalih svojstava.
	                        </para>
		        </listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odabir paketa prema stanju, odjeljku, nazivu ili prilagođenom filteru.
	                        </para>
		        </listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
	                                 Razvrstavanje paketa prema nazivu, stanju, veličini ili inačici.
		                </para>
			</listitem>
	                <listitem>
		                <para>
			                Pregledavanje sve dostupne dokumentacije s interneta povezane s paketom.
				</para>
	                </listitem>
	                <listitem>
		                <para>
			                Zaključavanje paketa na trenutnu inačicu.
				</para>
	                </listitem>
	                <listitem>
		                <para>
			                Odabir instalacije određene inačice paketa.
				</para>
	                </listitem>
		</itemizedlist>
	</para>
	<note>
		<para>
			Potrebne su vam korijenske ovlasti za instalaciju ili uklanjanje paketa softvera na vašem računalu.
		</para>
	</note>

	<para>
		Možete pokrenuti <application>&app;</application> na 
		sljedeće načine:

	<variablelist>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				U <guimenu>Cinnamon</guimenu> izborniku
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite 
					<menuchoice>
					<guisubmenu>Administracija</guisubmenu>
					<guimenuitem>Synaptic upravitelj paketa</guimenuitem>
					</menuchoice>. 
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				U <guimenu>GNOME 3</guimenu> Aktivnosti izborniku
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite 
					<menuchoice>
					<guisubmenu>Prikaži aplikacije</guisubmenu>
					<guisubmenu>Sve</guisubmenu>
					<guimenuitem>Synaptic upravitelj paketa</guimenuitem>
					</menuchoice>. 
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				U <guimenu>GNOME 2</guimenu> Aplikacije izborniku
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite 
					<menuchoice>
					<guisubmenu>Alati sustava</guisubmenu>
					<guimenuitem>Synaptic upravitelj paketa</guimenuitem>
					</menuchoice>. 
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				U <guimenu>KDE</guimenu> izborniku
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite 
					<menuchoice>
					<guisubmenu>Postavke</guisubmenu>
					<guisubmenu>Dodatno</guisubmenu>
					<guimenuitem>Synaptic upravitelj paketa</guimenuitem>
					</menuchoice>. 
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				U <guimenu>Mate</guimenu> Aplikacije izborniku
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite 
					<menuchoice>
					<guisubmenu>Alati sustava</guisubmenu>
					<guimenuitem>Synaptic upravitelj paketa</guimenuitem>
					</menuchoice>. 
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>U naredbenom redku</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Za pokretanje <application>&app;
					</application> iz naredbenog redka upišite:
				</para>
				<para>
					<command>synaptic</command> 
				</para>
				<para>
					Zatim pritisnite tipku <keycap>Return</keycap>
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
	</variablelist>
	</para> 

	<caution>
		<para>
			Synapticom možete učiniti vaš sustav neupotrebljivim.
		</para>
		<para>
			Synaptic vam omogućuje izvođenje promjena na jezgri vašeg sustava.
		</para>
		<para>
			Uvijek koristite &app; s oprezom.
		</para>
	</caution>

</sect1>

					</entry>
				</row>

					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Statusna traka</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje opće informacije o stanju  
						&app;.
					</entry>
				</row>
			</tbody>
		</tgroup>
	</table>

<sect2 id="synaptic-win-properties" xreflabel="svojstva">
	<title>Svojstva paketa</title>

	<para>
		Dijalog svojstva odabranog paketa možete otvoriti na sljedeće načine:
		
		<itemizedlist>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu><guimenuitem>Svojstva</guimenuitem></menuchoice>
				</para>
			</listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Kliknite na <guibutton>Svojstva</guibutton> u alatnoj traci
				</para>
			</listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki <keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>O</keycap></keycombo>
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</itemizedlist>
	</para>
		

	<figure>
		<title>Dijalog svojstva paketa</title> 
		<screenshot> 
			<mediaobject> 
				<imageobject>
					<imagedata fileref="figures/synaptic-packagedetails.png" 
						format="PNG"/> 
				</imageobject>
				<textobject> 
					<phrase>
						Prikazuje pojedinosti paketa u glavnom prozoru &app; main window.
					</phrase>
				</textobject> 
			</mediaobject> 
		</screenshot> 
	</figure>

	<para>
		Dijalog svojstva paketa omogućuje sljedeće kartice:

		<table frame="topbot" id="table-synaptic-propertiestabs">
			<title>Kartice u dijalogu svojstava</title>
			<tgroup cols="2" colsep="0" rowsep="0">
				<colspec colname="COLSPEC0" colwidth="30*"/>
				<colspec colname="COLSPEC1" colwidth="70*"/>
				<thead>
					<row rowsep="1">
						<entry valign="top" align="left">
							Kartica
						</entry>
						<entry valign="top" align="left">
							Opis
						</entry>
					</row>
				</thead>
				<tbody>
					<row>
						<entry valign="top">
							Informacije
						</entry>
						<entry valign="top">
							Prikazuje osnovne informacije paketa: naziv, stanje, održavatelja, 
							prioritet, izvor, oznake <emphasis>(ako su podržane)</emphasis> i inačice.
						</entry>
					</row>
					<row>
						<entry valign="top">
							Zavisnosti
						</entry>
						<entry valign="top">
							Prikazuje popis zavisnih, ovisnih, omogućenih, sukobljenih, predloženih i preporučenih paketa.
						</entry>
					</row>
					<row>
						<entry valign="top">
							Instalirane datoteke
						</entry>
						<entry valign="top">
							Prikazuje putanje datoteka instaliranog paketa na sustavu.
						</entry>
					</row>
					<row>
						<entry valign="top">
							Inačice
						</entry>
						<entry valign="top">
							Prikazuje dostupne inačice paketa za instalaciju.
						</entry>
					</row>
					<row>
						<entry valign="top">
							Opis
						</entry>
						<entry valign="top">
							Prikazuje opširniji opis paketa, zapis promjena i sliku paketa.
						</entry>
					</row>
				</tbody>
			</tgroup>
		</table>
	</para>
</sect2>

</sect1>

<sect1 id="synaptic-single"> 
	<title>Upravljanje paketima</title> 

	<para>
		Ovo poglavlje obuhvaća osnovne radnje <xref linkend="anchor-installing"/>,
		<xref linkend="anchor-removing"/> i <xref linkend="anchor-upgrading"/>
		paketa.
	</para>
	<para>
		Nadalje izvođenje <xref linkend="anchor-dist-upgrade"/>,
		<xref linkend="anchor-dwww"/> dokumentacije na internetu, 
		<xref linkend="anchor-changelog"/> zapisa promjena i naprednih radnja
		<xref linkend="anchor-force"/> instalacije određene inačice paketa,
		<xref linkend="anchor-lock"/> paketa na trenutnu inačicu su opisani.
	</para>  

<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-install"> 
	<title>Instalacija paketa</title>

	<anchor id="anchor-installing" xreflabel="instalacija"/>

	<note>
		<para>
			Ako je dostupna drugačija inačica istog paketa &app;  će uobičajeno odabrati 
			najprikladniju inačicu. Kako bi odabrali instalaciju određene inačice drugačije 
			od uobičajene, pogledajte <xref linkend="synaptic-pkg-force"/>.
		</para>
	</note>

	<para>
		Ako želite instalirati određeni paket obavite sljedeće korake:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Učitajte informacije paketa kako bi bila dostupna najnovija inačica:

			<itemizedlist>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
						<guimenuitem>Osvježi informacije o paketima</guimenuitem>
						</menuchoice>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Osvježi</guibutton>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>R</keycap></keycombo>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
			</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite paket za instalaciju:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Dvostruko kliknite na naziv paketa u
							<xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
		                        <listitem>
			                        <para>
				                        Kliknite na ikonu stanja paketa i odaberite u izborniku <guimenuitem>
					                Odaberi za instalaciju</guimenuitem>.
						</para>
	                                </listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Desno kliknite na paket u sadržaju izbornika i odaberite <guimenuitem>
							Odaberi za instalaciju</guimenuitem>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
			                <listitem>
				                <para>
					                Odaberite paket pa zatim iz
							glavnog izbornika odaberite <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu>
						        <guimenuitem>Odaberi za instalaciju</guimenuitem> </menuchoice>.
	                                        </para>
		                        </listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite paket i
							pritisnite kombinaciju tipki <keycombo>
							<keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>I</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
			<para>
				Ako instalacija paketa zahtijeva dodatne promjene, biti ćete upitani 
				za potvrdu. Kako bi isto odabrali dodatne promjene kliknite na tipku <guibutton>Odaberi
				</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Primijenite odabrane promjene za instalaciju paketa:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.	
					</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika  <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Primijeni odabrane promjene</guimenuitem> </menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Biti ćete upitani za potvrdu. Provjerite sažetak promjena koji će se primijeniti. 
				Za nastavak trenutne instalacije potvrdite promjene klikom na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Tijekom procesa promjena vidjet ćete traku napretka.
				To može potrajati neko vrijeme ovisno o broju promjena.
				Nakon završetka biti ćete vraćeni u glavni prozor.
			</para>				
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-remove"> 
	<title>Uklanjanje paketa</title>
	<anchor id="anchor-removing" xreflabel="uklanjanje"/>

	<para>
		<note>
			<para>
				Podešavanja paketa i korisnički stvoreni podaci (npr. web stranica u "/var/www") 
				nisu uklonjeni iz sustava po zadanome.
			</para>
			<para>
				<emphasis>Samo za Debian:</emphasis> možete promijeniti uobičajeno ponašanje u osobitostima.
			</para>
			<para>
				<emphasis>Samo za Debian:</emphasis> kako bi uklonili sve datoteke povezane s paketom
				odaberite <menuchoice><guimenuitem>Odaberi
				za potpuno uklanjanje</guimenuitem></menuchoice> umjesto 
				<menuchoice><guimenuitem>Odaberi za uklanjanje</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
			</para>
		</note>
	</para>
	<!-- para>
		<note>
			<para>
				<emphasis>Debian only:</emphasis> To also remove packages that are only installed as dependency 
				of the to be removed 
				package(s) and that would be no longer needed (orphaned) afterwards, choose 
				<menuchoice><guimenuitem>Remove Including Orphaned Dependecies</guimenuitem>
				</menuchoice> instead of <menuchoice><guimenuitem>Remove</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
			</para>
		</note>
	</para -->
	<para>
		Kako bi uklonili pakete slijedite ove korake:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite paket za uklanjanje:
				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Dvostruko kliknite na naziv instaliranog paketa u
							<xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
		                        <listitem>
			                        <para>
				                        Kliknite na ikonu stanja paketa i odaberite u glavnom izborniku <guimenuitem>
					                Odaberi za uklanjanje</guimenuitem>.
						</para>
	                                </listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Desno kliknite na paket i odaberite iz sadržajnog izbornika <guimenuitem>
							Odaberi za uklanjanje</guimenuitem>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika paket
							a zatim odaberite <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Odaberi za uklanjanje</guimenuitem>
							</menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite paket i pritisnite
							tipku <keycap>Delete</keycap>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
			<para>
				Ako uklanjanje paketa zahtijeva dodatne promjene, biti ćete upitani 
				za potvrdu. Kako bi odabrali dodatne promjene kliknite na tipku <guibutton>Odaberi
				</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Primjenjivanje odabranih promjena za trenutno uklanjanje paketa:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Primijeni odabrane promjene</guimenuitem> </menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Upitani ste za potvrdu. Provjerite sažetak promjena koje će se primijeniti. 
				Za nastavak trenutne instalacije potvrdite promjene klikom na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Tijekom primijenjivanja promjena vidjet ćete traku napredka. Pričekajte dok
				se promjene ne primijene. To može potrajati neko vrijeme ovisno o broju
				promjena. Nakon završetka biti ćete vraćeni u glavni prozor.
			</para>				
			<!--<note>
			 	<para>
					Traka napredka nije dostupna na Debian sustavima. Umjesto ćete
					vidjeti opširniji prikaz u terminalu.
				</para>
			</note> -->
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
</sect2>
<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-upgrade"> 
	<title>Nadogradnja paketa</title>

	<anchor id="anchor-upgrading" xreflabel="nadogradnja"/>

	<para>
		Za nadogradnju paketa slijedite ove korake:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Osvježite informacije paketa kako bi imali dostupne najnovije inačice:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Osvježi informacije o paketima</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Osvježi</guibutton>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>R</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>

		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite pakete za nadogradnju:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Dvostruko kliknite na naziv paketa s najnovijom inačicom dostupnom
							u <xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
				                        Kliknite na ikonu stanja paketa i odaberite iz izbornika <guimenuitem>
			                                Odaberi za nadogradnju</guimenuitem>.
						</para>
	                                </listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Desno kliknite na paket i odaberite iz izbornika sadržaja <guimenuitem>
							Odaberi za nadogradnju</guimenuitem>.
						</para>
		                        </listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite paket a zatim iz glavnog
							izbornika odaberite <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Odaberi za nadogradnju</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite paket i
							pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
			<para>
				Ako nadogradnja paketa zahtijeva dodatne promjene, biti ćete upitani 
				za potvrdu. Kako bi odabrali dodatne promjene kliknite na tipku <guibutton>Odaberi
				</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Primijenite odabrane promjene za nadogradnju paketa:
			</para>
			<itemizedlist>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
						<guimenuitem>Primijeni odabrane promjene</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
			</itemizedlist>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Biti ćete upitani za potvrdu. Provjerite sažetak promjena koji će se primijeniti. 
				Za nastavak trenutne instalacije potvrdite promjene klikom na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Tijekom primijenjivanja promjena vidjet ćete traku napredka. Pričekajte dok se
				promjene ne primijene. To može potrajati neko vrijeme ovisno o broju
				promjena. Nakon završetka biti ćete vraćeni u glavni prozor.
			</para>				
			<!-- <note>
				<para>
					The progressbar is not available on Debian system. Instead you will
					see a detailed terminal output.
				</para>
			</note> -->
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
	<tip>
		<para>
			Za nadogradnju svih instaliranih paketa na najnoviju inčicu,
			pogledajte <xref linkend="synaptic-pkg-upgradesystem"/>.
		</para>
	</tip>
</sect2>


<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-upgradesystem"> 
	<title>Nadogradnja cijelog sustava</title>

	<anchor id="anchor-dist-upgrade" xreflabel="nadogradnje sustava"/>
	
	<para>
		&app; omogućuje dva načina odabira paketa za nadogradnju:
		<variablelist>
			<varlistentry>
				<term>
					Zadana nadogradnja
				</term>
				<listitem>
					<para> 
						Način zadane nadogradnje odabire samo nadogradnju instaliranih paketa. 
						Ako najnovija inačica zavisnosti paketa zavisi o neinstaliranim paketima 
						ili je u sukobu s već instaliranim paketom, nadogradnja se neće odabrati.
					</para>
				</listitem>
			</varlistentry>
			<varlistentry>
				<term>
					Pametna nadogradnja (Dist-Upgrade)
				</term>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Način pametne nadogradnje pokušava inteligentno razriješiti sukobe paketa. To uključuje
						instalaciju dodatnih potrebnih paketa i preferira pakete s višim prioritetom.
					</para>
					<para>
						Pametna nadogradnja još je poznata i kao <emphasis>dist-upgrade</emphasis> u apt-get alatu terminala.
					</para>
				</listitem>
			</varlistentry>
		</variablelist>

		<note>
			<para>
				Nadogradnja na glavno izdanje operativnog sustava mora se obaviti načinom 
				pametne nadogradnje, npr. s Conectiva 9 na Conectiva 10 ili s 
				Debian Woody na Debian Stretch.
			</para>
		</note>
	</para>

	<para>
		Kako bi nadogradili svoj sustav na najnovije izdanje slijedite ove korake:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				 Osvježite informacije paketa kako bi imali dostupne najnovije inačice:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Osvježi informacije o paketima</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Osvježi</guibutton>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki <keycombo>
							<keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>R</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>

		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite sve dostupne nadogradnje: 

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Odaberi sve nadogradnje</guibutton>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Odaberi sve nadogradnje...</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki <keycombo>
							<keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>G</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite način nadogradnje. Preporučljiva je pametna nadogradnja.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Primijenite odabrane promjene za trenutnu nadogradnju paketa:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite u glavnom izborniku <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Primijeni odabrane promjene</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Biti ćete upitani za potvrdu. Provjerite sažetak promjena koji će se primijeniti. 
				Za nastavak trenutne instalacije potvrdite promjene klikom na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Tijekom primijenjivanja promjena vidjet ćete traku napredka. Pričekajte dok se
				promjene ne primijene. To može potrajati neko vrijeme ovisno o broju
				promjena. Nakon završetka biti ćete vraćeni u glavni prozor.
			</para>				
			<!--  <note>
				<para>
					The progressbar is not available on Debian system. Instead you will
					see a detailed terminal output.
				</para>
			</note> -->
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>

	<tip>
		<para>
			Možete promijeniti zadani način nadogradnje za buduće nadogradnje u osobitostima
			<!-- xref linkend="synaptic-prefs-actions"/-->.
		</para>
	</tip>
</sect2>

<!-- sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-downgrade"> 
	<title>To Downgrade Packages</title> 

	<caution>
		<para>
			This may render your package system unusable!
		</para>
		<para>
			Downgrading packages is a broken feature, since apt and &app; can only handle
			conflicts and dependency resolution of upgrades. 
		</para>
		<para>
			You may need this feature in some rare circumstances, but it is not recommended to use. 
		</para>
	</caution>

	<para>
		To downgrade a package you have to mark a specific version of the package:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Select the package that you wish to downgrade in the 
				<xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Open the Properties dialog of the package:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Click on <guibutton>Properties</guibutton> in the toolbar.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Choose <menuchoice><guimenu>Package</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Properties</guimenuitem></menuchoice> 
							from the menu.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Press the key combination 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Activate the <guibutton>Expert</guibutton> tab in the properties dialog. You will
				see a list of all available version of the package.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Choose the version to be installed from the list and close the Properties dialog.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Apply the marked changes to actually downgrade the package(s):

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Click on <guibutton>Apply Changes</guibutton> in the toolbar.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Choose <menuchoice><guimenu>Edit</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Apply Marked Changes</guimenuitem></menuchoice> 
							from the menu.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Press the key combination 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>

		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>

<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-apply">
	<title>Primijenjivanje odabranih promjena</title>

	<tip>
		<para>
			Za prikaz paketa koji su odabrani
			odaberite filter "Odabrane promjene", pogledajte 
			<xref linkend="synaptic-filter-apply"/>.
		</para>
	</tip>

	<para>
		Za primjenu odabranih promjena odaberite:

		<itemizedlist>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
				</para>
			</listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
					<guimenuitem>Primijeni odabrane promjene</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
				</para>
			</listitem>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
					<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</itemizedlist>
	</para>
	<para>
		Biti ćete upitani za potvrdu. Provjerite sažetak promjena koji će se primijeniti. 
		Za nastavak trenutne instalacije potvrdite promjene klikom na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.
	</para>
	<para>
		Tijekom primijenjivanja promjena vidjet ćete traku napredka. Pričekajte dok se
		promjene ne primijene. To može potrajati neko vrijeme ovisno o broju
		promjena. Nakon završetka biti ćete vraćeni u glavni prozor.
	</para>				
	<!-- <note>
		<para>
			The progressbar is not available on Debian system. Instead you will
			see a detailed terminal output.
		</para>
	</note> -->
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-undo">
	<title>Uklanjanje odabira promjena</title>

	<para>
		Sljedeći načini omogućuju vam uklanjanje odabira promjena:
	</para>
	
	<variablelist>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				Za uklanjanje odabira određenih paketa
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Desno kliknite na paket i odaberite iz sadržajnog izbornika <menuchoice>
							<guimenuitem>Ukloni odabir</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite paket a zatim iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice>
							<guimenu>Paket</guimenu><guimenuitem>Ukloni odabir</guimenuitem>
							</menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite paket i pritisnite kombinaciju tipki <keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap>
							<keycap>N</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				Za uklanjanje svih odabira
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
					<guimenuitem>Ukloni sav odabir</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				Za uklanjanje posljednjeg odabira
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
					<guimenuitem>Vrati</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
				</para>
				<para>
					Ovaj korak se može ponavljati. Najveći broj radnji vraćanja može biti postavljen u osobitostima.
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
	</variablelist>
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-config">
	<title>Podešavanje paketa (samo za Debian)</title>

	<para>
		<note>
			<para>
				Za korištenje ove značajke morate instalirati paket
				<emphasis>libgnome2-perl</emphasis>.
			</para>
		</note>
	</para>	

	<para>
		Debian omogućuje jedinstveni način, koji se zove <emphasis>debconf</emphasis>, za podešavanje softverskih paketa. Ova značajka
		nije podržana u svim paketima. Ako paket podržava ovu značajku podešavanje se može obaviti 
		putem pomoćnika.
	</para>

	<para>
		Za podešavanje podržanih paketa:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite paket koji želite podesiti u 
				<xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu>
				<guimenuitem>Prilagodi...</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
	<tip>
		<para>
			Za dobivanje popisa svih podesivih paketa odaberite iz odjeljka Prilagođeni filtri <emphasis>Debconf paketi</emphasis>.
		</para>
	</tip>
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-docu">
	<title>Pregledavanje dokumentacije paketa (samo za Debian)</title>

	<anchor id="anchor-dwww" xreflabel="pregledavanje"/>


	<note>
		<para>
			Za korištenje ove značajke morate instalirati paket <emphasis>dwww</emphasis>.
		</para>
	</note>
	
	<para>
		dwww omogućuje pristup svoj dokumentaciji instalacije
		paketa putem web preglednika. Sva dokumentacija je
		dostupna putem lokalnog HTTP poslužitelja.
	</para>

	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite paket o kojemu želite pročitati dodatnu dokumentaciju u 
				<xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu>
				<guimenuitem>Pretraži dokumentaciju</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>

</sect2>
<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-changelog">
	<title>Pregledavanje zapisa promjena paketa (samo za Debian)</title>

	<anchor id="anchor-changelog" xreflabel="čitanje"/>

	<note>
		<para>
			Mogu se vidjeti samo zapisi promjena izvornih Debianovih paketa.
		</para>
	</note>
	
	<para>
		Za pregled zapisa promjena izvornih Debianovih paketa obavite sljedeće korake:
	</para>

	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite paket u <xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu>
				<guimenuitem>Preuzmi zapis promjena</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-lock">
	<title>Zaključavanje paketa na trenutnu inačicu (samo za Debian)</title>

	<anchor id="anchor-lock" xreflabel="zaključavanje"/>

	<para>
		Za zaključavanje paketa na trenutnu inačicu slijedite ove korake:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite paket koji želite zaključati u <xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu>
				<guimenuitem>Zaključaj inačicu</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
			</para>
			<para>
				&app; će ponovno učitati informacije paketa. Tada bi trebali vidjeti, da je stavka izbornika <menuchoice>
				<guimenu>Paket</guimenu><guimenuitem>Zaključaj inačicu</guimenuitem></menuchoice> odabrana.
				Nadalje sve radnje u izborniku <guimenu>Paket</guimenu> sada su onemogućene.
			</para>
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
	<para>
		Za otključavanje paketa uklonite odabir iz izbornika sa <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu><guimenuitem>Zaključaj inačicu</guimenuitem>
		</menuchoice>.
	</para>
</sect2>



<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-force">
	<title>Odabir instalacije određene inačice</title>

	<anchor id="anchor-force" xreflabel="odabir"/>

	<warning>
		<para>
			&app; uvijek odabire najprihvatljivije dostupne inačice. Ako odaberete 
			drugačiju inačicu od zadane, mogu se dogoditi greške u rukovanju sa
			zavisnostima.
		</para>
	</warning>

	<para>
		Za odabir instalacije inačice paketa drugačije od zadane 
		obavite sljedeće korake:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Osvježite informacije paketa kako bi imali dostupne najnovije inačice:

			<itemizedlist>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
						<guimenuitem>Osvježi informacije o paketima</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Osvježi</guibutton>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
				<listitem>
					<para>
						Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>R</keycap></keycombo>.
					</para>
				</listitem>
			</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite paket s <xref linkend="synaptic-win-list"/>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Paket</guimenu><guimenuitem>Odaberi inačicu...</guimenuitem>
				</menuchoice> za otvaranje dijaloga sa svim dostupnim inačicama paketa.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite određenu inačicu za instalaciju. Za potvrdu vaše 
				odluke kliknite na tipku <guibutton>Odaberi inačicu</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Primijenite odabrane promjene za instalaciju odabrane inačice paketa:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.	
					</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite u izborniku <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Primijeni odabrane promjene</guimenuitem>
							</menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>P</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Biti ćete upitani za potvrdu. Provjerite sažetak promjena koji će se primijeniti. 
				Za nastavak trenutne instalacije potvrdite promjene klikom na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Tijekom procesa promjena vidjet ćete traku napretka.
				To može potrajati neko vrijeme ovisno o broju promjena.
				Nakon završetka biti ćete vraćeni u glavni prozor.
			</para>				
			<!-- <note>
				<para>
					The progressbar is not available on Debian system. Instead you will
					see a detailed terminal output.
				 </para>
			</note> -->
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
</sect2>
<sect2 id="synaptic-pkg-fix">
	<title>Popravljanje slomljenih paketa</title>
	
	<para>
		&app; neće dopustiti sve buduće promjene na sustavu prije nego što se svi slomljeni paketi ne poprave.
	</para>
	<tip>
		<para>
			Za prikaz svih slomljenih paketa odaberite u odjeljku Prilagođeni filtri <emphasis>Slomljeno</emphasis>.
		</para>
	</tip>

	<para>
		Za popravak slomljenih paketa odaberite sljedeće korake:
		odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
		<guimenuitem>Popravi slomljene pakete</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
	</para>

	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu><guimenuitem>Popravi slomljene pakete</guimenuitem>
				</menuchoice>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Primijenite odabrane promjene za trenutni popravak:

				<itemizedlist>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>.	
					</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
							<guimenuitem>Primijeni odabrane promjene</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
					<listitem>
						<para>
							Pritisnite kombinaciju tipki 
							<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>E</keycap></keycombo>.
						</para>
					</listitem>
				</itemizedlist>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Biti ćete upitani za potvrdu. Provjerite sažetak promjena koji će se primijeniti. 
				Za nastavak trenutne instalacije potvrdite promjene klikom na <guibutton>Primijeni</guibutton>
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Tijekom procesa promjena vidjet ćete traku napretka.
				To može potrajati neko vrijeme ovisno o broju promjena.
				Nakon završetka biti ćete vraćeni u glavni prozor.
			</para>				
			<!-- <note>
				<para>
					The progressbar is not available on Debian system. Instead you will
					see a detailed terminal output.
				 </para>
			</note> -->
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
</sect2>


</sect1>

<sect1 id="synaptic-repo">
	<title>Repozitoriji</title>

	<para>
		Paketi su dostupni putem takozvanih <emphasis>repozitorija</emphasis>. Repozitoriji sadrže
		pakete i sadržaj koji uključuje neke osnovne informacije o paketima, npr. potrebne zavisnosti	
		i kratak opis.
	</para>
	<para>
		Repozitoriji mogu biti smješteni na raznim medijima: CD-ROM/DVD-ima, lokalnim čvrstim diskovima, Internetu (http,ftp) 
		ili udaljenim datotečnim sustavima (nfs).
	</para>


<sect2 id="synaptic-repo-update"> 
	<title>Osvježavanje informacija paketa</title> 
	<para>
		Za prikupljanje popisa svih dostupnih paketa iz repozitorija odaberite jedan od sljedećih
		načina:
	</para>
	<itemizedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu>
				<guimenuitem>Osvježi informacije o paketima</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Kliknite u alatnoj traci na <guibutton>Osvježi</guibutton>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
	</itemizedlist>
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-repo-manage"> 
	<title>Uređivanje, dodavanje ili uklanjanje repozitorija</title> 
	<para>
		Možete uređivati, dodavati ili uklanjati repozitorije u osobitostima repozitorija.
	</para>
	<para>
		Otvorite uređivača repozitorija iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Postavke</guimenu>
		<guimenuitem>Repozitoriji</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
	</para>

	<figure>
		<title>Osobitosti repozitorija</title>
		<screenshot> 
			<mediaobject> 
				<imageobject>
					<imagedata fileref="figures/synaptic-repositories.png" 
						format="PNG"/> 
				</imageobject>
				<textobject> 
					<phrase>
						Prikazuje osobitosti repozitorija
					</phrase>
				</textobject> 
			</mediaobject> 
		</screenshot> 
	</figure>

</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-repo-cdrom"> 
	<title>Dodavanje repozitorija s CD-ROM-a</title> 
	<para> 
		Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Uredi</guimenu><guimenuitem>Dodaj CD...</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
	</para>
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-repo-aptline"> 
	<title>Sintaksa APT redka</title> 
	<para> 
		Upravitelj paketima sprema sve dostupne repozitorije u tekstom temeljenu datoteku podešavanja. 
		Svaki redak opisuje vrstu, lokaciju i sadržaj određenog repozitorija.
	</para>

	<table frame="topbot" id="table-synaptic-aptline-syntax">
		<title>Primjer sintakse APT redka: <emphasis>deb http://debian.iskon.hr stretch main</emphasis></title>
		<tgroup cols="2" colsep="0" rowsep="0">
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC0" colwidth="30*"/>
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC1" colwidth="70*"/>
			<thead>
				<row rowsep="1">
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						Element
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						opis
					</entry>
				</row>
			</thead>
			<tbody>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						deb
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prvi element opisuje vrstu repozitorija.
						Moguće vrijednosti su <emphasis>deb</emphasis> (Debian binarni paketi), <emphasis>deb-src
						</emphasis>(Debian paketi izvornog kôda), <emphasis>rpm</emphasis> (RPM binarni paketi), 
						<emphasis>rpm-src</emphasis> (Redhat paketi izvornog kôda), <emphasis>rpmdir</emphasis>
						(mape koje sadrže RPM binarne pakete) i <emphasis>rpmdir-src</emphasis> 
						(mape koje sadrže RPM pakete izvornog kôda).
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						http://debian.iskon.hr
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Drugi element je lokacija repozitorija.
						Repozitoriju se može pristupiti putem protokola HTTP i 
						FTP, ili lokalno na CD-u, DVD-u ili čvrstom disku.
					</entry>
			        </row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						stretch
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Treći element opisuje distribuciju za koji je 
						paket napravljen, pogledajte <ulink 
						url="https://www.debian.org/doc/manuals/developers-reference/ch04.en.html#s4.6.4">
						Napomene Debianovih razvijatelja</ulink> za više pojedinosti o Debianovim distribucijama.
					</entry>
			        </row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						main
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Četvrti element opisuje odjeljak 
						repozitorija koji bi trebao biti uključen. Prema zadanome službeni Debianovi repozitoriji su
						odvojeni u odjeljke <emphasis>main</emphasis>, <emphasis>
						contrib</emphasis> i <emphasis>non-free</emphasis>, pogledajte 
						<ulink
						 url="https://www.debian.org/doc/manuals/developers-reference/ch04.en.html#archive-sections">
						 Napomene Debianovih razvijatelja</ulink> za više pojedinosti o Debianovim odjeljcima.
					</entry>
			        </row>
			</tbody>
		</tgroup>
	</table>
	<para>
		<note><para>Distribucija i element odjeljka nisu potrebni za svaku vrstu.</para></note>
	</para>
</sect2>

</sect1>

<sect1 id="synaptic-filter">
	<title>Prilagođeni filtri</title>
	<para>
		Filtri vam omogućuju sužavanje popisa prikazanih paketa prema raznim svojstvima paketa.
	</para>
	<para>
		&app; dolazi sa skupom predodređenih filtra.
	</para>

	<table frame="topbot" id="table-synaptic-filters">
		<title>Predodređeni filtri</title>
		<tgroup cols="2" colsep="0" rowsep="0">
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC0" colwidth="30*"/>
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC1" colwidth="70*"/>
			<thead>
				<row rowsep="1">
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						Filter
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						Opis
					</entry>
				</row>
			</thead>
			<tbody>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Svi paketi</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje sve dostupne pakete.
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Odabrane promjene</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje sve pakete koji su odabrani za promjenu.
					</entry>
			        </row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Debconf paketi</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje sve pakete koji se mogu podesiti putem debconfa
						(samo za Debian).
			                </entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Slomljeno</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo pakete sa slomljenim zavisnostima.
			                </entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Nadogradivo (upstream)</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje sve pakete koji su nadogradivi na najnoviju 
						inačicu.
			                </entry>
				</row>
			</tbody>
		</tgroup>
	</table>


	<para>
		Zbog mogućih promjena u sučelju postavke predodređenih filtera su isto promijenjene.
		Stari i zastarjeli filtri nisu automatski uklonjeni Synapticom. 
	</para>

	<table frame="topbot" id="table-synaptic-filters-deprecated">
		<title>Zastarjeli filtri</title>
		<tgroup cols="2" colsep="0" rowsep="0">
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC0" colwidth="30*"/>
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC1" colwidth="70*"/>
			<thead>
				<row rowsep="1">
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						Filter
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						Opis
					</entry>
				</row>
			</thead>
			<tbody>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Instalirano</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo trenutno instalirane pakete.
					</entry>
			        </row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Nije instalirano</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje pakete koji nisu trenutno instalirani.
			                </entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Nadogradivo</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo pakete s najnovijom dostupnom inačicom.
			                </entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Slomljeno</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo pakete sa slomljenim zavisnostima.
			                </entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Programirane promjene</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo pakete odabrane za promjenu.
			                </entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Novo u arhivi</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo nove pakete od posljednje 
						nadopune repozitorija.
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Preostalo</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo uklonjene pakete koji su ostavili
						podatke ili datoteke podešavanja na sustavu.
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Debconf paketi</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo pakete koji se mogu podesiti putem
						debianovog sustava podešavanja paketa, takozvanog debconfa.
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Zastarjeli ili lokalno instalirani</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje samo pakete koji nisu uključeni (duže vrijeme)
						ni u jedan određeni repozitorij.
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">Filter pretrge</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Prikazuje rezultate posljednje pretrage paketa.
					</entry>
				</row>
			</tbody>
		</tgroup>
	</table>

<sect2 id="synaptic-filter-apply"> 
	<title>Primjenjivanje filtera</title> 
	<para>
		Za primjenu filtra slijedite ove korake:
	</para>
	<orderedlist>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite <guibutton>Prilagođeni filtri</guibutton> iz polja kategorija u 
				glavnom prozoru.
			</para>	
		</listitem>
		<listitem>
			<para>
				Odaberite željeni filter iz <xref linkend="synaptic-win-selector"/>.
			</para>
		</listitem>
	</orderedlist>
</sect2>

<sect2 id="synaptic-filter-edit" xreflabel="uređivač filtra"> 
	<title>Uređivanje i stvaranje prilagođenih filtra</title> 

	<para>
		Filtri se mogu stvarati, brisati i mijenjati u uređivaču filtra:
	</para>

	<para>
		Odaberite iz glavnog izbornika <menuchoice><guimenu>Postavke</guimenu><guimenuitem>
		Filtri</guimenuitem></menuchoice>.
	</para>

	<figure>
		<title>Uređivač filtra</title> 
		<screenshot> 
			<mediaobject> 
				<imageobject>
					<imagedata fileref="figures/synaptic-filter.png" 
						format="PNG"/> 
				</imageobject>
				<textobject> 
					<phrase>
						Prikazuje uređivača filtra.
					</phrase>
				</textobject> 
			</mediaobject> 
		</screenshot> 
	</figure>

	<para>
		Uređivač filtra sadrži sljedeće komponente:
	</para>
	
	<variablelist>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				Popis filtra
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Prikazuje popis dostupnih filtra.
				</para>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
		<varlistentry>
			<term>
				Pravila
			</term>
			<listitem>
				<para>
					Pravila vam omogućuju definiranje koji će paketi biti prikazani u popisu.
					Paket mora ispuniti sve odabrane kriterije.
				</para>
				<variablelist>
					<varlistentry>
						<term>
							Stanje
						</term>
						<listitem>
							<para>
								Omogućuje pretragu paketa prema stanju.
							</para>
						</listitem>
					</varlistentry>
					<varlistentry>
						<term>
							Odjeljak
						</term>
						<listitem>
							<para>
								Omogućuje pretragu paketa prema odjeljku,
							</para>
						</listitem>
					</varlistentry>
					<varlistentry>
						<term>
							Svojstva
						</term>
						<listitem>
							<para>
								Omogućuje pretragu paketa prema različitim
								svojstvima npr. nazivu, zavisnosti ili izvoru.
							</para>
						</listitem>
					</varlistentry>
					<varlistentry>
						<term>
							Ključne riječi
						</term>
						<listitem>
							<para>
								Ključne riječi su temeljene na deb-oznakama. Samo GNU/Linux 
								s instaliranim "deb-oznakama" podržava ovu značajku.
							</para>
						</listitem>
					</varlistentry>
				</variablelist>
			</listitem>
		</varlistentry>
	</variablelist>

</sect2>
</sect1>

<sect1 id="synaptic-bugs">
	<title>Poznate greške i ograničenja</title>
	<para>
		

	<table frame="topbot" id="synaptic-bugs-table">
		<title>Poznati problemi i njihova rješenja</title>
		<tgroup cols="2" colsep="0" rowsep="0">
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC0" colwidth="30*"/>
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC1" colwidth="70*"/>
			<thead>
				<row rowsep="1">
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						Problem
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top" align="left">
						Rješenje
					</entry>
				</row>
			</thead>
			<tbody>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Nepodržana lokalizacija
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						Koristite lokalizaciju samo podržanu Xlib-om.
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Neuspjeli instalacijski blokovi budućih radnji u Synapticu
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<para>
							Pod nekim rijetkim okolnostima trenutna instalacija ili uklanjanje paketa mogu biti neuspješni. 
							Kao posljedica sve odabrane promjene su isto tako prekinute. 
						</para>
						<para>
							&app; zahtijeva 
							čisto okruženje bez napola instaliranih paketa 
							za obavljanje dodatnih promjena. Ali trenutno ne postoji način za nastavak
							prekinutih instalacija sa &app;.
						</para>
						<para>
							Kako bi popravili ovu situaciju upišite sljedeću naredbu u terminal, zatim pritisnite tipku
							<keycap>Return</keycap>:
						</para>
						<command>
							apt-get install -f
						</command>
					</entry>
				</row>
			</tbody>
		</tgroup>
	</table>
	</para>
</sect1>

<sect1 id="synaptic-shortcuts">
	<title>Prečaci tipkovnice</title>
	<para>
		Popis svih općih prečaca u &app;:
	</para>
	<informaltable frame="none" id="synaptic-shortcuts-table">
	
		<tgroup cols="2" colsep="0" rowsep="0">
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC0" colwidth="30*"/>
			<colspec colname="COLSPEC1" colwidth="70*"/>
			<tbody>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Osvježi popis poznatih paketa
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>R</keycap></keycombo>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Otvori dijalog pretrage paketa
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>F</keycap></keycombo>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Otvori daijalog svojstva za odabrani paket
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>O</keycap></keycombo>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Odaberi odabrane pakete za instalaciju
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>I</keycap></keycombo>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Odaberi odabrane pakete za nadogradnju
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>U</keycap></keycombo>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Odaberi odabrane pakete za uklanjanje
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycap>Delete</keycap>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Odaberi odabrane pakete za potpuno uklanjanje (samo Debian)
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycombo><keycap>Shift</keycap><keycap>Delete</keycap></keycombo>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">
						Ukloni sve promjene sa odabranih paketa
					</entry>
					<entry valign="top">
						<keycombo><keycap>Ctrl</keycap><keycap>N</keycap></keycombo>
					</entry>
				</row>
				<row>
					<entry valign="top">

		a sada ga održava Michael Vogt (<email>mvo@debian.org</email>) i
		Gustavo Niemeyer (<email>niemeyer@conectiva.com</email>).
		Kako bi dobili više informacija o &app;, posjetite 
		<ulink url="https://github.com/mvo5/synaptic/wiki" type="http">Synaptic web stranicu</ulink>.
	</para>
	<para>
		Za prijavak greške ili prijedloga za &app; 
		ili ovog priručnika, koristite sustav prijave grešaka na <ulink 
		url="https://github.com/mvo5/synaptic/wiki" type="http">Synaptic web stranici</ulink>, kontaktirajte 
		nas na mailing listi <email>synaptic@nongnu.org</email> ili se pridružite IRC kanalu 
		#synaptic na irc.freenode.org.
	</para>

		Opće Javne Licence objavljene od strane Fundacije slobodnog softvera; ili inačice 2 licence, 
		ili (po vašem mišljenju) novije inačice. Kopija ove licence može se pronaći u
		, datoteci uključenoj u izvornom kôdu ovog programa.
	</para>
